# Graph Report - Nanoai  (2026-08-17)

## Corpus Check
- 2991 files · ~5,806,476 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 54744 nodes · 134528 edges · 1509 communities (1297 shown, 212 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 85% EXTRACTED · 15% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 19536 edges (avg confidence: 0.78)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `553c123e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
